langcurriculum › epistemics, argument, and teaching › source_reliability_learning

#95 source_reliability_learning

conditional, per-domain reliability

level 95section viiisource_modellinginductioncalibration
4.0 answers per episode
0.25 uniform-guess floor
0.145 constant-guess floor
100 samples below

What it generates

Reliability is conditional, not a scalar.

Each source has a *per-domain* accuracy, shown only as an itemized track record of past adjudicated claims. The query names a domain and asks who to trust there. Trial counts differ across sources, so the raw number of correct calls is not the answer; and in half the episodes the best source overall is deliberately not the best source in the queried domain, which is exactly the distinction a single global score cannot make.

Difficulty axes

axislevel
reasoning_depth3
uncertainty3
world_complexity4
